        function clock() {
            var now = new Date();
            var outStr = now.getHours() + ':' + now.getMinutes() + ':' + now.getSeconds();
            if (document.getElementById('clockDiv')) {
                document.getElementById('clockDiv').innerHTML = outStr;
            }
            setTimeout('clock()', 1000);
        }
        clock();
    </script>

        <script language="JavaScript" type="text/javascript">
            var obj = JSON.parse(jQuery("#form\\:records").val());

            jQuery().ready(function() {
                jQuery('#close').bind('click', function() {
                    closePopup('bin');
                });
                jQuery('#mellatClose').bind('click', function() {
                    closePopup('mellatBin');
                });
                jQuery('#mellatBin').jqm({modal: true,  trigger: false});
                jQuery('#bin').jqm({modal: true,  trigger: false});

                obj.forEach(function(e){
                        var chart = new FusionCharts(e.chartName , "ChartId"+e.order, e.width, e.height, "0", "1");
                        chart.render("chartdiv"+ e.order);
                        chart.setDataURL("/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType="+e.channelType+e.chartType+e.mode+e.allBank+"&channelId="+ e.id);
                        //chart.render("chartdiv"+ e.order);
                        if (e.allBank==1) {
                            /*Banks Response Time*/
                            var bankResponseTime = new FusionCharts("MSCombi2D", "BankResponseTimeId", "450", "350", "0", "1");
                            // bankResponseTime.setDataURL("");
                            bankResponseTime.render("bankResponseTimeDiv");

                            /*Banks Transaction Count*/
                            var bankTransactionCount = new FusionCharts("/nims/resources/fusionChart/MSLine.swf", "BankTransactionCountId", "450", "350", "0", "1");
                            // bankTransactionCount.setDataURL("");
                            bankTransactionCount.render("bankTransactionCountDiv");
                        } else if ((e.channelType == 9) && (e.mode == 2)  && (e.allBank==0)) {
                                /*Mellat Response Time*/
                                var chartMellat = new FusionCharts("MSCombi2D", "ChartMellatId", "450", "350", "0", "1");
                                // chartMellat.setDataURL("");
                                chartMellat.render("chartdivMellat");

                        }
                });

                jQuery(this).everyTime(30000, 'controlled', function() {
                    obj.forEach(function(e){
                            updateChart('ChartId'+ e.order,'/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType='+e.channelType+e.chartType+e.mode+e.allBank+"&channelId="+ e.id);
                    });
                });

            });

            function updateChart(DOMId, url) {
                console.log(DOMId);
                var chartObj = getChartFromId(DOMId);
                console.log(chartObj);
                chartObj.setDataURL(url);

            }

            function openBankPopup(bin, bankName, allBank, channelType) {
                    if (bin != 610433 || (bin == 610433 && allBank == 1)) {
                        jQuery(this).oneTime(700, 'controlledChart', function() {
                            updateChart('BankResponseTimeId', '/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType='+channelType+'7'+'1000' + bin);
                            updateChart('BankTransactionCountId', '/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType='+channelType+'2'+'1000' + bin);
                        });
                        jQuery('#bin').jqmShow();
                        jQuery('#bin').jqDrag('');

                    } else if (bin == 610433 && channelType == 9 && allBank==0) {
                            jQuery(this).oneTime(700, 'controlledChart', function() {
                                updateChart('ChartMellatId', '/nims/faces/report/online.chart?channelType='+channelType+'7'+'2000' + bin);

                            });
                            jQuery('#mellatBin').jqmShow();
                            jQuery('#mellatBin').jqDrag('');
                            jQuery.post("MarqueServlet", {B_Method:"bankName",bin:bin}, function(xml) {
                                jQuery('#mellatPoId').text(jQuery("bankName", xml).text());
                            });
                    }

               }

    document.onkeydown = function(e) {
        if (e == null) { // ie
            keycode = event.keyCode;
        } else { // mozilla
            keycode = e.which;
        }
        if (keycode == 27) { // escape, close box
            closeAllLOVS();
            alertDialog.hide();
        }
    };

    function fireAlerts() {

        if (jQuery("#form\\:hasAlert").val() == "true") {

            makeSound();

            try {
                alertDialog.show();
            } catch(err) {
            }

        } else {

            document.getElementById("beep").innerHTML = "";
            if (jQuery("div[aria-labelledby='ui-dialog-title-form:alertT1']").css('display') != 'none') {
                try {
                    alertDialog.hide();
                } catch(err) {
                }
            }


        }


    }

     function menuSelectEvent(value) {
         document.getElementById("form:selectedMenu").value = value;
         menuSelectAction();
     }


     function makeSound() {
        document.getElementById("beep").innerHTML = '<embed src="/resources//songs/beep-1.mp3" type="application/x-mplayer2" height="0" width="0" playcount="3"></embed>';
    }
